use fmin_fmax_functions

    real(8), intent (in) :: b

    real(8), intent (in) :: e

    if (c <= (-1d-52)) then
        tmp = g * -(c * e)
    else if (c <= 1d-42) then
        tmp = a * ((e * i) - (f * h))
    else
        tmp = c * (d * h)
    end if
    code = tmp
end function

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      \[\leadsto a \cdot \left(e \cdot i - f \cdot h\right) \]

                                                                                                                                                                                                                    2. Taylor expanded in e around inf to order 1

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      \[\leadsto a \cdot \left(e \cdot \left(i + -1 \cdot \frac{f \cdot h}{e}\right)\right) \]
                                                                                                                                                                                                                    3. Step-by-step derivation

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      1. Applied rewrites61.3%

                                                                                                                                                                                                                        \[\leadsto a \cdot \left(e \cdot \left(i + -1 \cdot \frac{f \cdot h}{e}\right)\right) \]

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      2. Taylor expanded in e around inf to order 0

                                                                                                                                                                                                                        \[\leadsto a \cdot \left(e \cdot i\right) \]
                                                                                                                                                                                                                      3. Step-by-step derivation

                                                                                                                                                                                                                        1. Applied rewrites52.1%

                                                                                                                                                                                                                          \[\leadsto a \cdot \left(e \cdot i\right) \]

                                                                                                                                                                                                                        if -9.9999999999999993e-125 < e < 9.9999999999999997e-155

                                                                                                                                                                                                                          3. Step-by-step derivation
